- **jue-literature-positioning**: Use when the contribution of a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript relative to the urban-economics frontier is fuzzy or undersold. - **jue-rebuttal**: Use when a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) decision letter (R&R or reject-and-resubmit) needs a response-letter strategy and a revision plan. - **jue-referee-strategy**: Use when anticipating the objections a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) referee will raise before submission, so the manuscript pre-empts them. - **jue-replication-package**: Use when assembling the data and code deposit for a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript under its mandatory replication policy — especially geocoded, restricted, or proprietary spatial data. - **jue-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a Journal of Urban Economics (JUE) manuscript via Elsevier Editorial Manager — submission fee, single-anonymized review, format, the JUE: Insights short-paper track, and the cover-letter confidential-data exemption. - **juri-perspective**: 西城樹里（Juri Saijo，偶像大师闪耀色彩·放課後クライマックスガールズ）的思维框架与表达方式。基于54+个日文来源（含SCTranslationData全量2229条台词程序化统计）的深度调研，提炼6个核心心智模型、10条决策启发式和完整表达DNA。 她是バッドガール——金发・口悪・被年下全员依赖的頼られ系年上：入社条件是「アタシのやり方を変えねー」，失败观是「全力だからこそ悔しい」，温柔是「もらってばっかじゃ返せてない」的负债偿还制，行动哲学是「いちばん後ろまで届ける」。 用途：作为思维顾问，用樹里的视角分析问题、审视决策，给出「不变条款→全力→恩返し→届ける」的行动者逻辑。 触发词：「用西城樹里的视角」「樹里会怎么看/怎么做」「樹里ならどうする」「如果是樹里」「扮演樹里」「以樹里的身份/口吻说话」「樹里式」「切换到樹里」「Juri perspective」「樹里ちゃん」；简体「树里」（樹里的简体同形）同样触发。单独点名「樹里/树里/西城/じゅり/Juri」（指闪耀色彩角色，中英日文均可）＋「怎么看/怎么想/思路/视角/建议/评价」等判断动词也触发；多个视角skill共存时被点名则参与竞逐。 不触发：仅闲聊卡面/新卡情报/他角色话题中顺带出现樹里而无视角意图；明确指其他作品或现实中的同名「樹里/Juri」；与夏葉等其他角色视角skill同场时，未被点名不参与，也不混入他… ([Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill](https://github.com/Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shinycolors/juri-perspective))
- **juri-plenario-sustentacao**: Atuacao no plenario do juri: instrucao em plenario (CPP 473-475 — vitima, testemunhas, interrogatorio), DEBATES (CPP 476-481 — acusacao, defesa, replica e treplica; tempo CPP 477 = 1h30 cada + 1h replica/treplica), vedacoes do CPP 478 (nao usar pronuncia, algemas ou silencio como argumento de autoridade), prova nova so com 3 dias uteis (CPP 479) e apartes (CPP 497 XII). Use quando o operador disser plenario, sustentacao oral juri, debates, replica treplica, instrucao em plenario. - **juri-recursos**: Recursos contra as decisoes do Tribunal do Juri: APELACAO do CPP 593, III (alineas a nulidade posterior a pronuncia; b sentenca contraria a lei/decisao dos jurados; c erro/injustica na pena; d decisao MANIFESTAMENTE CONTRARIA A PROVA DOS AUTOS), com devolutividade RESTRITA a alinea, novo juri so na alinea d (CPP 593 §3) e SOBERANIA DOS VEREDICTOS (CF 5 XXXVIII c). Trata tambem a execucao provisoria da pena &gt;=15 anos (CPP 492 I e + Tema 1.068).
